# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
$$\mathbf{s''(t)} \text{ is tangent to } \mathbf{s'(t)} \text{ if curvature is big, so}$$ ^yoUjWJc0

1) We need to measure how much they are orthogonal: **cross product** ^yY6BFPkG

$$\mathbf{s'(t)} \times \mathbf{s''(t)} = ^Q2kNMEzr

\begin{vmatrix} ^4zAPNFyl

x'(t) & x''(t) \\ ^rD4oEySy

y'(t) & y''(t) ^W87vBsOr

\end{vmatrix} ^TkCmBPqh

= x'(t) y''(t) - x''(t) y'(t)$$ ^fxrEYmFm

2) However, we need to normalize them. ^JHpPYMtf

$$\frac{\mathbf{s'(t)}}{\|\mathbf{s'(t)}\|} \times \frac{\mathbf{s''(t)}}{\|\mathbf{s'(t)}\|}$$ ^fHAziWp9

3) It is $( \frac{dT}{dt} )$, but we want to find $( \frac{dT}{ds})$. ^7hw1Ml1U

$$ ^guColHQq

\Rightarrow \text{ We need to divide by } \left\| \frac{ds}{dt} \right\| \text{ which is } \|\mathbf{s'(t)}\| ^7GJvb5LF

$$ ^jsYO8Ck9

4) ^KYmo6AbA

$$\frac{\mathbf{s'(t)} \times \mathbf{s''(t)}}{\|\mathbf{s'(t)}\|^3}$$ ^8M1rNnlh
